S34 2026-09-09 feature (design 002 slice 1.1): same worktree/branch. Replaced the dead
  `resyncActions` reconnect emit (N2 — no listener since slice 8) with a real requestPeerLog/
  peerLog pair: reconnecting client asks its peer for the selfActionData tail past its
  oppCounter, capped at 200 (PEER_LOG_MAX), replayed through the existing pushActionQueue
  chain; past-cap or no answer within 5s falls through to the O2-C "reload and rejoin"
  announcement. New DOM-free client/src/setup/netcode/peer-log-catchup.js (11 tests);
  pushAction handler refactored onto a shared applyPeerAction so live traffic and catch-up
  replay run identical code. server.js relays the two new events. 1004 tests green; changed
  files lint clean; test:2p green against a live server. Fixed a stale MAP.md section still
  listing slice-8-deleted reconciliation files as present.
  flag: design edge-case rows 6-7 ticked; row 7 (5s timeout) has no test harness for socket
        timers — verified by reading the guard, not by an automated test.

  array — same pattern as the existing takePrizesByIndex. Whole-zone ops (shuffleZone,

S45 2026-09-09 debug/re-gate(netcode slice 3.4e): traced `undo` per D6 ("deterministic commandLog
  replay minus tail against seed") before building it, per the slice's own "confirm or STOP"
  instruction. Found the premise broken: deck load (`initializePlayerDeck`, shadow.mjs:29-70)
  mints instanceId and populates `zones.deck` directly on `gameRoom.state` from a raw pushAction
  in server.js:665-698 — entirely outside `applyCommand`/`commandLog` — and `setupGame` reads
  `zones.deck`, not `deckList`. Replaying `commandLog` alone from a fresh seeded state therefore
  reconstructs empty decks, not the pre-undo game. No code changed. Filed I16, re-gated 3.4e in
  NEXTSTEPS.md pending a user call: (A) fold deck-load into a proper logged command, (B) replay
  bootstrap that re-mints decks from `deckList` in player-registration order before replaying the
  log, or (C) snapshot-based undo instead of event-sourcing. No branch, no commit, no PR.

S46 2026-09-09 feature(netcode slice 3.4e): user picked option A. Added `loadDeck` command
  (shared/engine/commands.mjs schema + DISPOSITION_TABLE entry N/A — internal-only, not a legacy
  client action; reduce.mjs case mints instanceId via the same draft.nextInstanceId counter as
  every other card-creating path, sets zones.deck + deckList, and — critically — runs through
  applyCommand so it lands in commandLog) and legality carve-out (loadDeck only allowed during
  turn.phase 'setup'). server.js's exchangeData/loadDeckData interception now calls
  gameRoom.handleCommand('loadDeck', ...) instead of mutating gameRoom.state directly via
  initializePlayerDeck (import removed, now unused). Implemented `undo` in reduce.mjs: rebuilds
  a fresh createGameState (players scaffolded from username only — zones/decks come back through
  replayed loadDeck commands), replays commandLog.slice(0, -count) through applyCommand with a
  fresh seeded rng, returns the replayed state directly (bypasses the common commandLog-push tail
  so undo itself is never logged — no undo-of-undo bookkeeping needed). Edge Case 18 (undo past
  log start) rejected `nothing_to_undo`, state untouched. Added dual-run-bridge.js translator
  case (undo -> {type:'undo',payload:{}}, ignoring legacy filteredActionData). Updated the stale
  DISPOSITION_TABLE.undo assertion in commands.test.mjs (disposition retired from 'undo' to
  'server_command'). New test file server/game/__tests__/undo-command.test.mjs (6 tests: loadDeck
  logging/instanceId minting, loadDeck phase gate, undo single/double revert incl. commandLog
  length + rngCursor equality, undo past log start, undo count exceeding log). Registered the new
  file in package.json's test script (explicit file list, not a glob). 1088/1088 tests green.
  flag: an `eslint --fix` sweep across the 6 touched files (meant to clean up my own new lines)
  reformatted entire pre-existing file contents (2890 insertions/363 deletions across reduce.mjs,
  commands.mjs, dual-run-bridge.js, server.js, and their tests) — no functional change, tests
  stayed green throughout, user chose to keep it rather than hand-revert. Be aware the working
  tree's whitespace/wrapping style in these 6 files no longer matches the rest of the untouched

S47+1 2026-09-09 fix(netcode I17): closed I17. First attempt (loosen moveCard's payload.from
  check in shared/engine/reduce.mjs) broke an existing named invariant test (Edge Case 3:
  stale_view) and didn't fully fix it anyway (card sets still diverged) — reverted clean,
  1088/1088 verified. Real fix: server is sole shuffle authority end to end — server.js emits a
  'dealOrder' event (per-player syncInstance order) once both decks are actually loaded, client's
  setupPrizes() waits for and uses it (client/src/setup/netcode/deal-order.js). Found a second bug
  while verifying manually in the browser: the 'setup' trigger's "both players present" guard
  fired on the FIRST loadDeck (both playerIds exist from addPlayer, before either has a deck),
  dealing one player an empty hand and sending them a 0-length dealOrder that then wiped their
  deck via rearrangeArray — fixed with an allDecksLoaded check. Verified via manual 2-tab browser
  session (javascript_tool) plus 3 fresh live-recorded fixtures through replay-harness.test.mjs,
  all green. Harness's hand-order mismatch (real cards, wrong order) traced to a pre-existing
  client-side hand auto-sort (move-card.js:604-607, cosmetic, already tracked as I15/design row
  10) — harness now compares zone membership order-insensitively (canonicalizeHash), not
  string-exact. replay-harness.test.mjs added to pnpm test's file list. 1089/1089 green.
  UNCOMMITTED per standing user instruction.

S49 2026-09-09 feature(netcode slice 3.7): counter/status overlay reconciliation. Added
  reconcileDamageOverlay/reconcileSpecialConditionOverlay to apply-view.js, called per-card
  after placeCardInZone: creates/positions/removes the damage-counter/status-marker sibling
  <div>s from cardData.damage/cardData.specialCondition on every applyView, reusing
  img.damageCounter/img.specialCondition as the storage slot (same field legacy
  addDamageCounter/addSpecialCondition use) and the existing shared style helpers
  (damage-counter-style.mjs, special-condition-style-apply.js) so tier/status classes stay
  identical to legacy. Display-only: no contentEditable/input/blur listeners — click-to-edit
  on an authoritative card still needs zoneArray population (same gap 3.6 flagged as open).
  Server stores conditions as full words (normalizeSpecialCondition); added a small word->code
  map at the render boundary since getSpecialConditionClass expects the short editable codes.
  Registry cleanup now also strips these overlays (zone-element siblings, not children of the

S70 2026-09-10 patch(holo): fixed dead Secret/Gold Rare holo effect. resolveHoloEffect() has
  mapped Secret/Gold/Shiny Rare to data-rarity="rare holo vmax" all along, but every CSS rule
  for that value only fired alongside [data-trainer-gallery="true"] (rainbow-alt.css), an
  attribute nothing in the codebase ever sets — these cards got zero holo overlay. New
  client/src/css/holo/gold-secret-rare.css targets [data-rarity="rare holo vmax"] directly.
  First attempt replaced the repeating-gradient sunpillar beams with a single non-repeating
  gradient, theorizing the repeat pattern aliased into noise at small size — wrong: user's
  video reference shows exactly the repeating-beam + glitter-sparkle look (marked 1/2/3 on
  three shifting color beams), and that same repeating-gradient technique is already used by
  regular-holo.css/hyper-rare.css elsewhere with no complaints, so the technique wasn't the
  issue. Reverted to mirroring hyper-rare.css's proven structure verbatim (sunpillar beams via
  ::before, exclusion diagonal-shift via ::after, --iri7/8/9 glitter-dust sparkle layer),
  retargeted to "rare holo vmax". Registered via @import in index.css after rainbow-alt.css.
  User verifies visually on localhost themselves going forward (feedback memory, project
  memory dir, not this repo).
  flag: not verified live this session — confirm the hyper-rare-mirrored version actually
  matches the reference video at real render sizes next session before considering this closed.
  If it's still called grainy, the earlier "bright background + color-dodge blows out" theory
  (from the first WRONG diagnosis) was never actually tested against this real structure —
  check that angle before reaching for a third rewrite.

  User then reported "just a circle going left and right instead of having the pillar" — real
  pre-existing repo bug, not a design issue: `--rotate-delta` is referenced in
  calc((var(--rotate-x) - var(--rotate-delta)) * ...) by hyper-rare.css, regular-holo.css,
  ex-special-illustration-rare.css, and the new gold-secret-rare.css, but holo.mjs never sets
  it and no CSS file defined it — an undefined custom property with no fallback invalidates
  the whole calc(), silently dropping every beam/pillar layer that depends on it. Only
  card__glare (driven by --pointer-x/y, which IS set) rendered — the "circle." Fixed with one
  line in base.css's shared :root/.card var block: `--rotate-delta: 0deg;` (no lag tracked, so
  0 is correct — not a workaround). This also silently repairs the same dead-pillar bug on
  Holo Rare / Hyper Rare / Special Illustration Rare cards elsewhere in the app, not just the
  new secret-rare CSS. NOT verified live — user checks localhost.

  User then reported "no pillars still, and the grain is too strong". This time debugged with
  real DOM inspection (dynamic-imported holo.mjs in-page, built a controlled test card with a
  synthetic solid-color canvas image since live TCGdex/pkmncards URLs 404'd in the sandboxed
  browser) instead of guessing again. Found: (1) a hard-reload was needed for --rotate-delta to
  actually take effect (confirmed via fetch of the served base.css — fine, just a stale-page
  false alarm); (2) a SECOND repo-wide undefined-var bug, same species as --rotate-delta:
  `var(--card-opacity)` is used inside calc() by base.css (with a `, 1` fallback — fine) but
  WITHOUT a fallback by every rarity CSS file (hyper-rare.css, regular-holo.css, rainbow-alt.css,
  ex-full-art.css, illustration-rare.css, ex-regular.css, reverse-holo.css, gold-secret-rare.css)
  — invalid calc() pins those opacities to their initial value (1, i.e. permanent max intensity)
  instead of the intended pointer-driven fade. Fixed with `--card-opacity: 1;` in base.css
  (matches the value the old pre-port index.css.bak used). (3) Opened iri-9.webp directly:
  it's dense fine-grain TV-static noise, not discrete sparkle stars — tiled at hyper-rare.css's
  150px with contrast(2)/plus-lighter that reads as solid grain, not "sparkles" (checked the raw
  asset, not assumed). Retuned gold-secret-rare.css only (did not touch the other rarity files
  sharing the same asset/technique, out of scope): --glitter-size 150px->480px (thins the same
  asset's visible density without replacing it), glitter filter contrast 2->1.1, glitter:before/
  after opacity formulas each *.5, shine:before (the beam layer) bumped filter contrast/saturate
  and switched overlay->color-dodge for a more distinct band edge. Verified the retune in the
  same synthetic-card DOM test: visibly a real shifting teal/white band, no longer solid static.
  Real card art will read differently (more hue variation than a flat test color) — user
  verifies the actual look on localhost, this was sandboxed verification only, not a final call.

  User: "bands aren't vertical". Rotated shine:before to 90deg (repeating-linear-gradient
  angle: bands run perpendicular to the gradient direction, so 90deg = left-to-right color
  change = vertical bands) and added a 90deg base offset to shine:after's two angles (they'd
  been left at ~0deg/rotate-x-only, i.e. horizontal, and that layer sits on top at z-index 3 —
  masking the vertical ::before layer underneath). Self-verified in a synthetic-card sandbox
  test, judged it fixed — wrongly: the browser's <link> stylesheet was cached from before this
  edit (a real page reload is required for CSS file changes to take effect; dynamic-importing
  the .mjs with a cache-busting query only refreshes the JS module, not the already-loaded
  CSS), so both my "fixed" read and the user's follow-up check likely ran stale CSS. User
  supplied a direct annotated screenshot proving the bands were still horizontal — caught my
  own misread this time instead of asserting it was fixed. Confirmed via `fetch` of the served
  file that the 90deg edit was written to disk correctly (it was); the actual gap was the
  never-reloaded page. Re-verified for real after a forced navigate+reload, and separately
  confirmed a flat-color synthetic test card is unreliable for this kind of check (color-dodge
  blending against an unnaturally saturated flat color can wash a layer out entirely, giving
  false negatives) — switched to real card art: the exact pkmncards.com Basic Water Energy
  asset already used by set-browser.mjs, then to the actual reference card (PAL 279/193 Secret
  Rare) via images.pokemontcg.io once the user supplied it, confirming the real card's own
  printed art already carries the sparkle-star look — the CSS layer only needs to add the
  moving sheen on top, not draw sparkles from scratch. Bands read correctly oriented against
  real art in the sandbox now. NOT a substitute for the user's own check — pointer-tracked
  motion doesn't show in a static screenshot the way it does hovering live in the app.
  flag: going forward, ANY CSS-file edit requires a forced page reload (not just a JS module
  cache-bust) before testing again in this browser sandbox — cost real time twice in this
  session (base.css --rotate-delta fix once, gold-secret-rare.css 90deg fix once) by skipping
  it. Also: a flat solid-color synthetic test card is unreliable for holo/blend-mode
  verification — use real card art (a working local asset or a confirmed-loadable remote URL)
  instead.

  User: still just a horizontal line after that fix. Re-read the file: card__shine:after (the
  black/gray "exclusion cross-hatch" secondary layer, copied verbatim from hyper-rare.css) was
  angled to 90deg+rotate-x last round but its background-position was left driving off
  --background-y — a vertically-banded pattern (90deg) doesn't visibly move when you shift its
  position along Y (the axis it's uniform along), so that layer rendered as a static artifact,
  and sitting on top at z-index 3 it was masking/dominating whatever card__shine:before (the
  actual vertical sunpillar layer) was doing underneath. Rather than keep patching two
  interacting layers, deleted card__shine:after outright and kept a single card__shine:before
  vertical-bar layer, position now driven by --background-x (the axis it actually varies
  along, so motion is visible) and background-size narrowed from 240% to 33% width so ~3 full
  color cycles are visible across the card at once (240%/70% both rendered as one broad soft
  wash edge-to-edge, not the several distinct bands the reference shows). Verified against the
  real PAL 279/193 card art (images.pokemontcg.io) after a confirmed hard-reload (checked the
  served file's bytes before testing this time) — screenshots at two different pointer
  positions show a real teal/cyan vertical band pattern that visibly shifted between them, not
  a static horizontal artifact. Cleaned up all test DOM nodes each round.

S74 2026-09-10 fix: KO'd Pokémon's attached Energy wasn't auto-discarding, and manually
  discarding it threw a false "energy already attached this turn". Cause: relocateAttachedCards
  dumped detached cards into the generic `attachedCards` staging zone even when the host's real
  destination was discard/lostZone — energy got stranded there, and rules-bridge.js's
  checkEnergyAdds (the only place that ever sets flags[user].energyAttached) misread its arrival
  as a fresh attach. Fix: new pure resolveDetachedCardDestination() routes straight to
  discard/lostZone when that's the host's destination; attachedCards unchanged for hand/deck.
  Legacy client-side rules-mode only — server-authoritative handleKnockout (reduce.mjs) was
  already correct. 3 new tests, 1211/1211 pnpm test green. Key files:
  relocate-attached-cards.js, resolve-detached-card-destination.js (new),

  (discardHandThenDraw needs none — it discards, not reshuffles). 1297/1297 pnpm test; targeted
  eslint on the touched file shows only pre-existing CRLF/prettier noise. Not live 2P verified
  (no harness yet for Judge/Iono-style effects) — fix mirrors design 007's already-verified
  await-the-move pattern. I36: traced the abort's blast radius (accept-action.js /
  socket-event-listeners.js's applyPeerAction) and confirmed oppCounter increments
  unconditionally regardless of the mirror-abort's return value — no desync, no side effect.
  Concluded working-as-intended, closed without a code change. Key files: trainer-execution.js.
  Both entries moved to ISSUES.md Closed with full reasoning. Pushed branch.
S99 2026-09-11 debug/fix(netcode): deck desync on room join. Cause: server replays the peer's
  cached exchangeData/loadDeckData right after emitting joinGame; the client's async joinGame
  handler awaited getProtocolVersion first, so the replay applied pre-isTwoPlayer (1P slot,
  unlogged) and was then wiped by cleanActionData('opp')+reset('opp'). Fix: joinGame setup runs on
  pushActionQueue (socket-event-listeners.js). Repro/regression: join-deck-sync-test.mjs (2/4 red -> 4/4).
  flag: two-player-sync-test.mjs fails at the 7-card hand wait on this branch with AND without the S99 fix (baseline, 2/2 runs) — pre-existing, untriaged.

S100 2026-09-11 patch(fix): Dawn's "search Basic/Stage1/Stage2 Pokémon" collapsed to one 3-pick
  step whose combined filter matched any Pokémon of any stage, letting the player take 3 Basics.
  Real card is 3 separate single-card searches, one per stage. New parser output type
  'searchDeckSequence' (trainer-effects.mjs, Dawn's card-text branch) with 3 stage-tagged sub-
  steps; new executor runSearchSequenceStep (trainer-execution.js) chains 3 single-card
  runSearchStep calls via suppressShuffle, then shuffles once at the end (matches "then shuffle
  your deck" appearing once in the printed text). search-match.mjs's matchesSearch previously had
  no stage-1/stage-2-only branch (fell through to "return true", matching ANY Pokémon) — added
  exact-stage filters. 2 new regression tests (parser shape + matchesSearch per-stage exactness).
  1299/1299 pnpm test green (was 1297). Key files: shared/engine/rules/trainer-effects.mjs,
  shared/engine/rules/search-match.mjs, client/src/setup/rules/trainer-execution.js.
S101 2026-09-11 fix(netcode/rules): user screenshot — fresh room showed "YOUR TURN — main phase",

  new 'room-changed' event (room-buttons.js, socket-event-listeners.js joinGame + server_restart)

  when the requester is alone, instead of relaying into an empty room and timing out. New

S102 2026-09-11 fix(netcode): user reported S101 did not fix Render. Render runs server-authoritative;
  S101 was verified in legacy only. Reproduced + fixed three server bugs: GameRoom kept the game after

  flag: flip-gate-test.mjs fails on main even with S102 stashed (I39) — the SA exit gate is red.
MERGE NOTE: renumbered S89 -> S90 on merge into claude/cpu-testing-live-render-anr7fy. Two

S90 2026-09-11 feature(bot): playtest bot now runs under SERVER_AUTHORITATIVE. Three gaps, in

S104 2026-09-12 patch: fixed 3 bugs behind "old-set imports show as Unknown, not Pokémon"
  (drafted as S91, renumbered on merge — main's own journal already reused S91 twice by the
  time this branch merged, see the two S91 entries above). (1) import.js:548-566
  `getOldCardType(tcgId)` unconditionally overwrote a correct `getCardType(set,number)` result
  whenever any tcgId resolved, no `!type||type==='Unknown'` guard (unlike the later
  Limitless-fallback block); now gated the same way. (2) `LEGACY_SET_CODE_TO_TCGDEX_ID`
  (legacy-set-ids.mjs) had zero entries for Black & White or HeartGold&SoulSilver short codes
  (BLW/EPO/NVI/NXD/DEX/DRX/DRV/BCR/PLS/PLF/PLB/LTR/LTRRC/BWP/HS/UL/UD/TM/CL/CLSL/HSP) — added,
  ids cross-checked against find-old-type-database.js's already-established
  bw1-11/hgss1-4/col1/dv1/bwp/hsp keys (ground truth already in this repo). (3)
  `getCardType`/`getOldCardType` breakpoint loops (find-type.js, find-old-type.js) had no
  catch-all past a set's last recorded breakpoint — the literal last card of any set (and
  anything beyond, e.g. a secret rare) always fell to 'Unknown'; now continues the last known
  type. +10 tests (find-type.test.mjs, find-old-type.test.mjs, legacy-set-ids.test.mjs
  additions). Not unit-tested at the import.js call-site itself (DOM-coupled module, no jsdom
  harness for it) — verified by static trace instead.
S105 2026-09-12 feature: deck builder summary bar (POKÉMON/TRAINERS/ENERGY counts) is now

S107 2026-09-12 patch(holo): closed the Secret/Gold/Shiny Rare holo saga started at S70. User
  pointed at a local checkout of the actual upstream project this repo's holo CSS was ported
  from (simeydotme's pokemon-cards-151/poke-holo) and said to take the gold-energy and "3 star
  rare" effects 1:1 from there instead of continuing to hand-tune. Diffed our CSS files against
  it wholesale: `hyper-rare.css`, `base.css`'s core structure, and
  `ex-special-illustration-rare.css` are ALL byte-identical 1:1 ports already; the other
  rarity files differ only in the documented "render unmasked" mask/clip-path stripping
  base.css's own header comment already calls out — no drift, no bugs there. Root cause of the
  entire S70 saga: `holo.mjs`'s RARITY_EFFECTS mapped Secret/Gold/Shiny Rare to
  `rare holo vmax`, but the reference source (Card.svelte:361, rainbow-alt.css:19/58/83/141)
  only styles `rare holo vmax` when `data-trainer-gallery="true"` — an attribute this app never
  sets (no Trainer Gallery concept ported). Gold-bordered cards should map to `hyper rare`
  instead, which already has full unconditional CSS. Every S70 "fix" was hand-rolling a new
  competing CSS file to approximate what the already-correct, already-in-the-repo
  hyper-rare.css does for free once pointed at the right selector. Fixed: `holo.mjs` (both the
  exact-match table and the substring fallback) now maps Gold/Secret/Shiny Rare to `hyper
  rare`; deleted the now-unnecessary `gold-secret-rare.css` and its @import in index.css (S70's
  base.css fixes for `--rotate-delta`/`--card-opacity` stay — those were real bugs, now
  confirmed correct by reading Card.svelte's JS: the reference sets both per-element via
  Svelte springs, `--rotate-delta` staying 0 during normal hover just like the S70 fallback,
  `--card-opacity` driven by a hover-fade our simpler always-on animation model has no
  lifecycle equivalent for — the flat `1` fallback is a deliberate, now-documented
  simplification of that, not a bug). Verified live: built both `hyper rare` (Secret Rare) and
  `special illustration rare` against real PAL 279/193 card art after a confirmed-fresh
  reload — both render with visible sparkle-dust and a shifting sheen.
  flag: ex-full-art.css also differs from the reference by dropped
  `[data-supertype="pokémon"]`/`[data-subtypes*="supporter"]` selector scoping on its Ultra
  Rare rules (broadens the effect to every Ultra Rare card regardless of supertype) —
  pre-existing, out of scope here, not touched. Worth knowing if Ultra Rare styling is ever
  reported as wrong.
  flag: this session repeatedly skipped hard-reloading the page after CSS edits before
  re-testing in the browser sandbox, wasting several rounds chasing phantom "still broken"
  results that were actually stale cache — see S70's own flag. Confirmed fixed via `fetch`ing
  the served file's bytes before every test in this final round; keep doing that.

S106 2026-09-12 feature (follow-up to S105): the same summary-bar filter now also narrows the
  Browse Sets panel's card grid, not just the deck list. TCGdex's `/sets/{id}` card list (what
  fetchSetCards used) carries no category field — only the per-card detail endpoint does, and
  fetching that per card would be one request per card — so added `fetchCardSupertypeIndex()`
  (set-browser.mjs): one lazy, cached-once id→supertype map built from three
  `/cards?category=Pokemon|Trainer|Energy` sweeps (same endpoint shape D18 already used for the
  Energy tab), tags every card `fetchSetCards` returns. Energy-tab cards (fetchLegalEnergyCards/
  fetchGenerationEnergyCards/getModernBasicEnergyCards) get `supertype: 'Energy'` directly, no
  lookup needed. New `filterCardsBySupertype()` applied alongside the existing name filter in
  native-deck-builder-set-browser.js's render(); new `setSupertypeFilter()` controller method,
  called from native-deck-builder.js's render() every time so Browse Sets always mirrors
  `deckListFilter`. +5 tests, pnpm test 1320/1320.

S108 2026-09-13 patch(holo): aligned holo spring physics and math 1:1 with simeydotme/pokemon-cards-151.
  Replaced single-point spring with 3 decoupled Svelte-style springs (springRotate, springGlare, springBackground),
  fixed inverted 1D --pointer-from-center with exact 2D Euclidean distance from center, enabled .card__glare2,
  and stabilized interactive card hover tracking. Added holo.test.mjs (8 tests), pnpm test 1328/1328.

S109 2026-09-13 fix(rules-ui): resolved Rare Candy GUI failing to advance to evolution selection.
  Root cause: openMatPick matched clicks via `e.img === event.target || e.img.contains(event.target)`.
  When cards in play are hydrated as holo cards, .mat-holo and .card__rotator place overlay sibling
  elements (.card__shine, .card__glare) directly over the <img> tag. Mouse clicks landed on
  .card__shine or .card__rotator instead of the bare <img>, causing hit-testing to never resolve a
  card, leaving "Cancel" as the only interactive button.
  Fix: extracted `buildMatPickEntry` and `findMatPickHit` into `client/src/setup/rules/mat-pick.mjs`.
  Hit-testing now checks the outer target element (retrieved via imageAnchor or card.wrapper),
  the bare img, and the parent .play-container. Also adjusted highlight styling to targetEl so
  card__rotator overflow:hidden does not clip the highlight. Tightened `evolveStage2` in
  `trainer-execution.js` to properly compute `wasPlayedThisTurn` from `base.enteredPlayTurn === rulesState.turnNumber`
  and ensure card metadata is loaded. Added unit tests in `mat-pick.test.mjs`.

S110 2026-09-13 fix(rules): support EX/ex Pokémon in evolution chains and stadium searches.
  Root cause: Pokémon card names containing "ex", "EX", "-EX" (e.g. "Charmander ex", "Charmeleon ex",
  "Charizard ex") failed exact-string equality against `evolvesFrom` (e.g. 'charmander' !== 'charmander ex').
  Rare Candy and Grand Tree searches failed similarly.
  Fix: added `cleanPokemonName(name)` and `pokemonNamesMatch(a, b)` in `shared/engine/rules/evolution.mjs`
  to canonicalize base species by stripping suffix tokens (ex, gx, vmax, vstar, v), accents, and non-alphanumerics,
  while safely preserving words beginning with "ex" (Exeggcute, Exeggutor, Excadrill, Exploud).
  Updated `resolveStage1EvolvesFrom` to look up cached data and TCGdex via clean base names.
  Updated `canEvolve` to use `pokemonNamesMatch` across normal evolution, Rare Candy stage-1 base resolution,
  and same-turn evolution markers. Updated `normalizeStage` to handle "Stage 1 ex", "Stage 2 ex", "Basic ex",
  and "MEGA". Updated `matchesStadiumEvolveSearch` in `stadium-effects.mjs` and Grand Tree logic in
  `chat-buttons.js` to match host Pokémon and Stage 2 candidates using `pokemonNamesMatch`.
  Updated `matchesSearch` in `search-match.mjs` to canonicalize card stages with `normalizeStage`.
  Excluded opponent evolve triggers from active damage-counter placement in `abilities.mjs`.
  Added tests in `evolution.test.mjs` and `rules-extended.test.mjs`. All unit tests green.

S111 2026-09-13 fix(rules-ui): resolved Rare Candy failing to evolve Swinub / mat clicks doing nothing.
  Root cause: In the 2-player browser client, playmat zones (active, bench) reside inside iframe documents
  (`selfContainerDocument` / `oppContainerDocument`). `openMatPick` in `trainer-execution.js` previously only
  attached `click` and `keydown` event listeners to the top-level parent `document`. Mouse events originating
  inside an iframe do not bubble across the iframe document boundary to the parent document, rendering all clicks
  on in-play cards (such as Swinub) completely dead. Secondarily, `evolveStage2` and `devolve` ended their switch
  cases with `break;` rather than `return;`. Because `openMatPick` is asynchronous, execution immediately fell
  through to `runAt(idx + 1)` and triggered `onComplete` (`discardTrainerFromBoard`), prematurely discarding Rare
  Candy ~40ms after hitting the board while waiting for user interaction.
  Fix:
  1. Updated `openMatPick` in `trainer-execution.js` to collect all target documents (`document`, `selfContainerDocument`,
     `oppContainerDocument`, iframe `contentWindow.document`, and candidate element `ownerDocument`s) and attach capture
     listeners to all of them, as well as direct capture listeners on each candidate's `targetEl`, `img`, and `container`.
  2. Maintained style restoration via a `Map` recording original `outline`, `cursor`, and `draggable` properties across
     both `targetEl` and `img`.
  3. Enhanced `findMatPickHit` in `mat-pick.mjs` to match attached cards and cards sharing a `.play-container`.
  4. Updated `evolveStage2` and `devolve` in `trainer-execution.js` to `return;` during user pick interaction and properly
     invoke `runAt(idx + 1)` upon pick confirmation, or `onComplete?.()` on cancellation or empty candidates.
  5. Added unit tests in `mat-pick.test.mjs` verifying attached energy hits, direct card matching, and multi-document event handling.
     All 1327 unit tests pass.

S112 2026-09-13 patch(abilities): fix search destination routing for "played onto bench" triggers (Meowth ex).
  Root cause: `parseAbilitySearchParams` matched `lower.includes('onto your bench')` across full ability text,
  falsely setting destination to 'bench' on "when you play this Pokémon ... onto your Bench" triggers (e.g. Last-Ditch Catch).
  Supporter/Item cards placed on bench were blocked by move-card.js, causing cards to stay in deck when shuffled.
  Fix: scoped search destination parsing to the search clause and stripped trigger phrasing; shared/engine/rules/abilities.mjs.
S113 2026-09-14 patch(rules): implement Iono prize-based draw and empty-hand draw prevention.
  Root cause: `ionoShuffle` previously only moved cards into deck and announced shuffle without drawing; also shuffled deck instead of keeping cards on bottom.
  Fix: updated `trainer-effects.mjs`, `trainer-execution.js`, and `executor.mjs` to put hands on deck bottom, draw cards equal to remaining prize cards for each player, and skip draw for player who played Iono when their hand was empty. Added unit tests in `trainer-execution.test.mjs`.

S114 2026-09-14 feature(deck-builder): added Reverse Holo energy variants for all available generations (Gen 3, 6, 7, 8).
  Added `REVERSE_HOLO_ENERGY_SET_IDS_BY_GENERATION` in `set-browser.mjs` mapping sets with printed reverse holo basic energies
  (Gen 8: Crown Zenith swsh12.5; Gen 7: SM Base sm1, Guardians Rising sm2, Burning Shadows sm3, Crimson Invasion sm4;
  Gen 6: Evolutions xy12, Generations g1; Gen 3: Expedition ecard1, EX Ruby & Sapphire ex1, EX Emerald ex9, EX Holon Phantoms ex13,
  EX Power Keepers ex16). Tested generations without reverse holos (Gen 5) to verify clean no-op. 1333/1333 unit tests pass.

S115 2026-09-14 feature(board-ui): aspect-ratio locked playmats with ambient bleed wings.
  Root cause: Playmats were forced to stretch via object-fit: fill into wide player halves (2.68:1+), squishing mat artwork and creating mismatch with standard card proportions.
  Fix: Locked mat profiles to physical aspect ratio (~1.91:1 single-player, ~1.0:1 two-player sheet) with centered layout and ambient blurred bleed backdrop on the table surface. Scaled horizontal zone coordinates in mat-layouts.mjs via CSS custom properties (--mat-aspect, --mat-width, --mat-offset-x). Updated apply-mat-layout.js and index.css to manage both ambient and foreground images. Added unit tests in mat-layouts.test.mjs.

S116 2026-09-14 feature(board-ui): expand #battleMat to 100% in fullscreen sidebox-hidden mode.
  Root cause: When closing the sidebox (.sidebox-hidden), .self and .opp expanded to 100% width while #battleMat was pinned at 75.5%, leaving an unstyled white margin on the right and misaligning cards with the mat.
  Fix: Expanded #battleMat to 100% under .sidebox-hidden; wired dynamic container variables (--mat-container-width, --mat-half-height) across index.css and container stylesheets. Mat and cards center in lockstep across the full monitor width.

S117 2026-09-14 fix(deck-builder): card preview right-click support on search tab and deck cards.
  Root cause: In the native deck builder, search result card buttons rendered by `renderSearchResults` in
  `native-deck-builder-renderers.js` lacked `data-card-id`, unlike `renderCardsGrid` in Browse Sets.
  Additionally, the search results contextmenu listener relied solely on `data-result-index` rather than
  `data-card-id` lookup, making card resolution fragile. Furthermore, deck list cards in `#nativeDeckBuilderCardsPanel`
  only handled `click` and lacked a `contextmenu` listener.
  Fix:
  1. Added `data-card-id="${escapeHtml(card.id)}"` to search result buttons in `renderSearchResults`.
  2. Updated `native-deck-builder.js` searchResults `contextmenu` listener to support both `[data-preview-image]`
     and `.native-deck-builder-result`, resolving the card via `data-card-id` with `resultIndex` fallback and
     resolving the preview image with multi-source fallback.
  3. Added `contextmenu` listener to `#nativeDeckBuilderCardsPanel` to enable right-click card preview on deck list cards.
  4. Verified via automated Playwright test that right-clicking opens floating 3D card preview popup with holo effect
     across Search Tab, Deck Panel, and Browse Sets. All 1333 unit tests pass.

S118 2026-09-14 fix(board-ui): synchronize #battleMat and card zones in side-menu-collapsed mode.
  Root cause: In Session 116, expansion was only applied to `.sidebox-hidden` (toggled by the fullscreen button in the pass/coin bar). But collapsing the right tab (#sideMenuToggle) sets `body.side-menu-collapsed`, which left `#battleMat` at 75.5% while .self and .opp were set to `calc(100% - 2vw)`. This created a 215px horizontal desync between mat artwork (centered inside 75.5vw) and card zones (centered inside 98vw), left an unstyled space on the right, and exposed closed deck builder workspace text peeking out from the screen edge.
  Fix:
  1. Updated `index.css` to include `body.side-menu-collapsed` in `--mat-container-width: 100vw`, `#battleMat: 100%`, and `#boardButtonContainer: left: 67%`.
  2. Changed `body.side-menu-collapsed .self, body.side-menu-collapsed .opp` to `width: 100%` so both mat and iframes share identical 100% container geometry.
  3. Added `transition: width 220ms ease;` to `#battleMat` to animate in lockstep with the iframes.
  4. Hid `.native-deck-builder-workspace:not(.open)` completely (`transform: translateX(100%); visibility: hidden; pointer-events: none;`).
  5. Updated `getPlaymatBounds()` in `card-picker.js` to recognize both `side-menu-collapsed` and `sidebox-hidden`.
  6. Verified all 46 unit tests pass.

S119 2026-09-14 feature(board-ui): edge-to-edge zoneless playmat profiles and custom Kyogre mat.
  Context: User provided a zoneless Kyogre illustration to test edge-to-edge display across the board.
  Changes:
  1. Added `EDGE_TO_EDGE` (one-player, `matFit: 'cover'`) and `EDGE_TO_EDGE_TWO_PLAYER` (two-player full board, `matFit: 'cover'`) profiles to `mat-layouts.mjs`, reusing `SIM.zones` so cards remain spread across the full board.
  2. Updated `apply-mat-layout.js` to toggle `mat-fit-cover` class on `#battleMat` when the active mat profile uses `matFit: 'cover'`.
  3. Added `#battleMat.mat-fit-cover` rules in `index.css`: disables `#battleMatTrim` / `#battleMatSurface` inset and polygon clipping (`inset: 0; clip-path: none; background: none; box-shadow: none;`), and sets `img.mat-art-image` to `width: 100%; height: 100%; object-fit: cover; border-radius: 0; box-shadow: none;`.
  4. Added `Playmat Kyogre (Edge-to-Edge)` and `Playmat Full size Kyogre (Edge-to-Edge Table)` to `MATS_CATALOG` with assets in `client/src/assets/playmats/custom/`.
  5. Updated mat picker layout labels to annotate edge-to-edge mats.
  6. Verified all 46 unit tests pass.

S120 2026-09-14 feature(board-ui): playmat zoom control and default 85% scale for edge-to-edge mats.
  Context: In edge-to-edge mode (cover), wide display aspect ratios (3.55:1) cropped 45%+ of vertical art height, making subjects appear overly zoomed in.
  Changes:
  1. Added `--mat-scale: 0.85` in `:root` and scaled edge-to-edge mat artwork via `transform: scale(var(--mat-scale, 0.85))`, pulling the artwork back to reveal more of the composition (head, fins, background).
  2. Enabled the `.mat-ambient-image` backdrop for edge-to-edge mats so exposed border space is smoothly filled with matching blurred ocean glow.
  3. Added interactive Zoom slider (50%–120%, default 85%) to Customize > Mat picker with localStorage persistence (`ptcg-sim.playmat.scale`).
  4. Verified all 46 unit tests pass.

S121 2026-09-14 feature(board-ui): playmat zones SVG overlay, zone alignment & remove low-opacity highlights.
  Context: Added vector zones SVG overlay (`playmat_zones_overlay.svg`) to render on clean playmat images, aligned card spaces to match SVG outlines, and eliminated low-opacity card space highlight boxes.
  Changes:
  1. Copied `out/playmat_outlines/playmat_zones_overlay.svg` into `client/src/assets/playmats/`.
  2. Updated `apply-mat-layout.js` to manage an `img.mat-zones-overlay` layer for active clean/custom playmats.
  3. Updated `index.css` to position and scale `img.mat-zones-overlay` in 1:1 lockstep with the mat artwork.
  4. Aligned `ONE_PLAYER` and `EDGE_TO_EDGE` zone coordinates in `mat-layouts.mjs` to match the exact SVG geometry (bench, active, prizes, deck, trash/discard).
  5. Updated `.outline`, `#lostZoneCover`, and `body.mat-active` in `self-containers.css` and `opp-containers.css` to remove low-opacity highlights and make card zone plates transparent.
  6. Verified all 46 unit tests pass.

S122 2026-09-14 feature(board-ui): overlapping prize cards layout matching playmat.
  Context: Reworked the prize cards layout so that cards on the left are mostly overshadowed by cards on the right, matching the playmat SVG's 120×163 px card outlines and vertical stagger.
  Changes:
  1. Updated `#prizes` in `self-containers.css` and `opp-containers.css` to use a 3-column CSS grid (`13.67% 72.66% 13.67%`) spanning 3 rows.
  2. Left cards (`:nth-child(2n+1)`) span columns 1/3 at `z-index: 1` with a `translateY(6.75%)` vertical stagger matching the SVG's offset back card rects.
  3. Right cards (`:nth-child(2n)`) span columns 2/4 at `z-index: 2`, sitting on top and overshadowing ~84% of the left cards, leaving only the 19px outer strip visible.
  4. Added `:hover { z-index: 10; }` so hovering any card temporarily lifts it to the foreground for easy viewing and interaction.
  5. Scoped `.prizes-normal-size` / `.prizes-small-size` in `#prizes` to `max-height: 100%` so row heights are cleanly respected.
  6. Verified all 60 unit tests pass.

S123 2026-09-14 patch: disable 3D tilt for holo cards on the board.
  Context: Holo cards placed on the board tilted in 3D during auto-sweep, causing perspective skew against 2D playmat slots.
  Changes:
  1. Updated `base.css` to add `transform: none;` on `.mat-holo .card__rotator`.
  2. Updated `index.css` to preserve 3D tilt specifically on card preview overlays and picker carousels.
  3. Added `tilt: !auto` option in `holo.mjs` and explicitly passed `tilt: false` in `hydrate-holo.js` and `close-popups.js`.
  4. Added regression unit test in `holo.test.mjs` (9/9 pass).

S124 2026-09-14 fix(board-ui): unify playmat and zone geometry for sub-pixel card alignment.
  Context: Fixed card space alignment misalignment where playmat zones were much bigger than existing card displays and slots drifted.
  Root Cause:
  1. `object-fit: cover` on `.mat-fit-cover` stretched the 1024×528 SVG overlay horizontally on wide arenas by 1.416×, squashing and magnifying card zones.
  2. The iframe was unscaled by `--mat-scale` and rendered in an independent bounding box, causing 60-100px drift from the SVG art.
  3. Trim/surface insets shrunk standard mats without adjusting the iframe.
  4. Counters (deck, discard, lost zone) were hardcoded to fixed offsets rather than tracking dynamic zone positions.
  Changes:
  1. `client/src/setup/sizing/mat-layouts.mjs`: Added `--mat-height`, `--mat-offset-y`, and `--mat-scale` computation; zones scaled via `scaleBottom` and `scaleV` while preserving exact aspect ratio.
  2. `client/src/css/index.css`: Replaced `object-fit: cover` with aspect-locked `--mat-width`, `--mat-height`, `--mat-offset-x`, `--mat-offset-y` layout for art, zones overlay, and playmat iframes in 1:1 geometry lockstep. Removed trim/surface insets when `.mat-active`.
  3. `client/src/css/self-containers.css` & `client/src/css/opp-containers.css`: Dynamic counter positioning relative to deck/discard/lost zones; transparent zone backgrounds.
  4. `client/src/setup/sizing/apply-mat-layout.js` & `native-deck-builder-mat-picker.js`: Synchronized `--mat-scale` across root and both child iframes on startup and slider change (default 100%).
  5. Verified via Playwright: sub-pixel alignment across all zones (<2px diff at 100% and 85% scale). All 1337 unit tests pass.

S125 2026-09-14 patch(board-ui): fix mat and card adjustment when right drawer is closed.
  Context: When the side drawer was closed, the playmat remained stuck at the 75.5vw offset (x=134px) while cards shifted to 100vw (x=291px), causing severe card misalignment.
  Root Cause: `--mat-container-width: 100vw` was scoped only to `body.side-menu-collapsed` / `body.sidebox-hidden`, but `--mat-offset-x` and `--mat-width` are declared on `document.documentElement` (`:root` / `<html>`), which never received the class or the 100vw override.
  Changes:
  1. `client/src/css/index.css`: Extended `--mat-container-width: 100vw` selector to `:root.sidebox-hidden`, `:root.side-menu-collapsed`, `:root:has(body.sidebox-hidden)`, `:root:has(body.side-menu-collapsed)`. Added `:root` selectors to layout rules. Added smooth 220ms ease transitions to mat art, zones overlay, and `#stadium`.
  2. `client/src/initialization/document-event-listeners/sidebox/side-menu-toggle.js` & `table/board-buttons.js`: Toggle collapsed / hidden classes on `document.documentElement` as well as `document.body`, and dispatch resize events.
  3. `client/src/setup/sizing/apply-mat-layout.js`: Synchronized layout CSS variables per-half to `.mat-half-self` and `.mat-half-opp`.
  4. `client/src/setup/sizing/__tests__/mat-layouts.test.mjs`: Added regression unit test verifying `:root` container width expansion rules. Verified via Playwright that relative zone positions match within 0.03% (<0.2px) across open, closed, reopened, and fullscreen states.

S126 2026-09-15 patch(board-ui): enhance playmat zones vector sharpness and edge contrast.
  Context: User reported mat outline SVG felt blurry when in use.
  Root Cause:
  1. SVG had fixed width="1024" height="528" creating an intrinsic raster resolution boundary in browser image pipelines.
  2. Pure white 1.8px stroke and 11px font had no contrast backing, causing sub-pixel anti-aliasing to wash out into light and complex playmat artwork.
  Changes:
  1. `client/src/assets/playmats/playmat_zones_overlay.svg` & `out/playmat_outlines/playmat_zones_overlay.svg`:
     - Removed fixed pixel width/height attributes; added `shape-rendering="geometricPrecision"` and `text-rendering="geometricPrecision"`.
     - Increased stroke-width to 2.2px for solid edge definition, yellow baseline to 2.4px, and font-weight to 800 (11.5px).
     - Added internal `<feDropShadow>` contrast filter (tight 0.8px halo + 1px drop-shadow) ensuring crisp visibility across light and dark artwork.
  2. Verified visually in browser via Playwright and confirmed all 1338 unit tests pass.

S131 2026-09-14 patch (cont.): user localhost look: zones on art OK; non-holo hand cards did not lift and the deck stack looked inverted. Cause: resetImage() (reset-image.js:22) sets an inline `transform: rotate(0deg)` on every card img, which beats the stylesheet's hover/cover transforms, so the unraised cover let the layers poke out above the card. Fix: use the CSS `translate` property (self/opp-containers.css). Verified in the pane: cover shifts 12.9px (self, up) and 12.6px (opp, local down = screen up) with the inline transform present; pnpm test 1402/1402.
  flag: user says the table "reads as multiple planes"; measured geometry is one plane (mat edges match both playfields within 1px). Meaning unresolved.
S131 2026-09-14 patch (cont.): "multiple planes" = the near half bulged toward the viewer (pivot + eye at the seam). Re-derived the tilt in table-tilt.mjs: pivot and eye at the near playfield's bottom edge (so the whole table recedes), then translateY(shift) so the projected seam stays on the iframe boundary (otherwise the far half's near-seam cards clip at its iframe edge); far half = flip-conjugate (origin D above its top, negated shift and angle). The #battleMat spread shadow fills the bands a receding table leaves. Pane measurement: seam y=455 on both halves, near edge 750px = flat, far edge about 704px, mat edges = playfield edges within 0.1px. pnpm test 1401/1401.
  flag: #battleMat box-shadow (20vh) is a taste call and may cover a custom page background; drop it if the user dislikes it.
S131 2026-09-14 patch (cont.): tilt 14 -> 12deg (user). Fuzzy cards: Chrome rasterizes any layer whose screen transform has perspective at 1 texel per CSS px, ignoring DPR (headless, DPR 1.5: sharpness 76 flat vs 8 tilted; will-change/backface-visibility no help). Fix: #playfield laid out at 2x via `zoom: 2` and shrunk by scale(0.5) inside the tilt transform (zoom also scales transform lengths, so table-tilt.mjs halves them; origin 0 0 with explicit pivot translates). Shipped code, headless DPR 1.5: sharpness 80.1 vs 10.3 without oversampling, same card size. Pane: seam/mat edges unchanged by the change. clientWidth -> style.width round-trip inside zoom is stable (attach/evolve/hydrate-holo pattern). pnpm test 1403/1403.
  flag: #battleMat mat art is still a 1x-rastered perspective layer (may look soft on HiDPI); the same oversampling would apply there. Drag/drop and offsetX-style coordinates inside the zoomed #playfield untested in a live game.

S132 2026-09-15 patch (cont.): energy slides in that carousel were showing the small round token
  icon (attach-card.js swaps the board <img> src to the icon and stashes the full card art in
  dataset.energyCardSrc for attached Energy). Fixed by building each Energy slide from a
  {...attached, image: {src: fullArtSrc}} stand-in instead of the live card object, so the
  carousel shows full art while open and the real board icon is untouched — it's already back to
  the icon on close since nothing on the DOM element was ever changed.
S132 2026-09-15 patch (cont.): attached cards were landing left of the main card instead of
  right. card-picker.js's computeSlideLayout uses `peekOffset = virtualIndex - slideIndex`
  (positive => right); candidates were [main, ...attached] with initialIndex 0, so attached
  (higher index) landed left. Fixed: candidates = [...attached, main], initialIndex =
  attached.length, so attached cards sit at lower indices than the focused main card.

S144 2026-09-16 debug: sleeves render grey in multiplayer when a deck is loaded/sleeve chosen after
  joining a room (fixed on rejoin). Root cause: changeCardBack (import.js) and applySleeveToPlaymat
  (native-deck-builder.js) both matched stale card-back images by comparing raw stored systemState
  values (relative paths, e.g. default '/src/assets/cardback.png') against live img.src (always
  browser-resolved absolute) - the comparison never matched, so an already-built deck cover was never
  repainted with the new sleeve; only a full buildDeck rebuild (rejoin) ever showed it. Fixed by
  resolving both sides to absolute URLs via the target iframe's own document before comparing.
  Key files: client/src/setup/deck-constructor/import.js, client/src/initialization/document-event-
  listeners/sidebox/native-deck-builder.js.
  flag: no automated test added - both functions are tightly coupled to live iframe DOM/systemState
  globals with no existing test scaffold; verified via code trace + a live 2-tab join/join repro that
  confirmed #deckCover renders as an empty div (no img) before any deck is loaded, matching the report.

S153 2026-09-17 patch: fixed holo not appearing on many hand/mat cards. hydrateHolo() (hydrate-holo.js)
  called ensureCardData({ name, type }) — dropping card.image/set/number — so id resolution skipped the
  deterministic image-URL->id path (extractTcgdexIdFromImageUrl) and fell back to fuzzy by-name search,
  which often failed silently, leaving card.rarity unset and resolveHoloEffect() returning null. Now
  passes id/name/type/set/number/image through. 112/112 targeted tests + 1583/1586 full suite green
  (3 pre-existing network-dependent "live:" failures confirmed unrelated via git stash comparison).

S166 2026-09-17 patch: audit B-1 closed — takePrizes was a bare `count <= prizes.length` check with no turn

S174 2026-09-18 feature: comprehensive server-authoritative parser sweep and rules engine parity (PR #149).
  - Ability & Tool decoupling: attachedTools resolves via attachedTo and recognizes card.isTool headless without DOM.
  - Effective HP & Knockout parity: effectiveHp integrated into all 4 KO check sites (active attack, recoil, bench damage, checkup) with Tool (+100 Hero's Cape) and Stadium HP buffs.
  - Attack execution: parsed.heal applies healing to attacker with damageUpdated event; status conditions and coin flips integrated.
  - Trainer play conditions & limits: 1 Stadium per turn enforced; bench checks for Switch (switchOwn) and Boss's Orders (switchOpponent); 5-bench limit for fossilItem; Rare Candy turn 1/2 and same-turn restrictions.
  - Evolution timing & legality (I59): per-card enteredPlayTurn and lastEvolvedTurn tracked in moveCard, attachCard, searchEvolve, and evolveStage2; attachCard legality validates turn 1/2, same-turn, once-per-turn evolved flag, stage sequence progression, and evolution line matching.
  - Stadium steps (I58): executeStadium branches for hand-to-deck-top, switch-type, discard-to-bench, and generic search fallback.
  - Executor ability steps: mapped discardCostAbility, statusAbility, and opponent gust switchAbility.
  - Classic and modern gust phrasing parity: matchesSwitchOpponentIn supports both SV Active Spot and SWSH/classic Active Pokémon wording.
  - 1807/1807 tests green across 42 suites. Closed I58, I59.

S175 2026-09-18 feature: Phase 1 combat passives & modifier pipeline under SERVER_AUTHORITATIVE (PR #149).
  - Damage Prevention: Mimikyu Safeguard, Cornerstone Ogerpon ex, and Tool prevention clamp attack damage to 0 and emit damagePrevented event.
  - Damage Reduction: Rock Chestplate (Fighting typing check), Radiant Gardevoir (V attacker check), and Tool damage reductions applied after Weakness & Resistance.
  - Attacker Tool Bonuses: Choice Belt (+30 vs V), Maximum Belt (+50 vs ex), Defiance Band (+30 when trailing on prizes), and Binding Mochi (+40 when poisoned) factored into attack damage before Weakness & Resistance.
  - Combat Thorns & Retaliation: Rocky Helmet and Druddigon Rough Skin ability apply retaliation damage to attacker after damage, emit damageUpdated (reason: 'thorns'), and trigger KO if lethal.
  - KO Prevention: Survival Brace leaves Pokémon with 10 HP, emits koPrevented event, and discards tool.
  - Prize Modifiers: Lillie's Pearl reduces prize cards taken by opponent on KO by 1; Briar grants +1 prize when a Tera Pokémon knocks out an opponent Active Pokémon ex.
  - Damage order of operations aligned in computeAttackDamage and reduce.mjs: Base -> Attacker Tools -> Weakness/Resistance -> Defender Reduction -> Prevention.
  - Added shared/engine/__tests__/combat-passives.test.mjs (13 tests) and wired into package.json test script. 1820/1820 tests green across 50 suites.

S176 2026-09-18 feature: Phases 2, 3, & 4 server-authoritative rules engine parity (PR #149).
  - Phase 2 (Retreat Engine & Tool Capacity):
    - computeEffectiveRetreatCost integrates printed base retreat cost, top evolution stats, attached Tools (Float Stone, Rescue Board, Air Balloon), bench abilities, and Stadium discounts (Beach Court).
    - Jamming Tower and tool-negating Stadiums suppress tool-granted free retreat or retreat discounts.
    - Stadium retreat prevention and turn locks validated in reduce.mjs.
    - Enforced 1-Tool-per-Pokémon attachment cap in validateLegality (attachCard), with dynamic support for extra tool slots via abilities (Genesect Tool Master).
    - Retreating or switching Pokémon between active and bench automatically clears Special Conditions and turn locks (cannotAttackUntilTurn, cannotAttackAttackName, cannotRetreatUntilTurn).
  - Phase 3 (Secondary Attack Effects & Costs):
    - Attack energy discard: parseAttackEnergyDiscard and execution in reduce.mjs supports typed energy discard ("Discard 2 Fire Energy from this Pokémon") and generic/all energy discard ("Discard all Energy attached to this Pokémon") with cardMoved events.
    - Next-turn attack and retreat locks: parseNextTurnLock parses "can't attack during your next turn", specific attack lock ("can't use [Attack Name] during your next turn"), and opponent retreat lock ("the Defending Pokémon can't retreat during your opponent's next turn"). Server validates locks in attack and retreat cases.
    - Attack deck search: parseAttackSearchClause generates authoritative draft.pendingChoice (searchDeck) on attacks like Call for Family, gates turn advance behind choice resolution, and resolves selected cards to bench or hand with deck shuffle in resolveChoice.
  - Phase 4 (Unlimited Energy Acceleration):
    - parseUnlimitedHandEnergyAcceleration parses abilities granting unlimited basic energy attachments from hand (Baxcalibur Supercold, Frosmoth Ice Dance, Blastoise, Magnezone).
    - reduce.mjs allows repeated hand energy attachment when an accelerator ability is active in play matching energy type and target constraints.
  - Added test suites shared/engine/__tests__/retreat-tool-cap.test.mjs (7 tests) and shared/engine/__tests__/attack-effects-execution.test.mjs (6 tests). Registered all new tests in package.json. 1851/1851 tests green across 58 suites. ESLint clean on all touched files.

S177 2026-09-18 feature: Starting Active Pokémon Selection Step (TCG Live Style).
  - Setup Flow Rework: Removed premature synchronous calls to `beginTurn()` and `updateTurnBanner()` in `beginSetupWithTurnOrder` in `rules-bridge.js`. Turn 1 now strictly begins only after both players' Active spots are populated.
  - Interactive Card Picker: After opening hands and mulligans resolve, `promptStartingActiveSelection` opens the TCG Live modal card picker (`openCardPicker` in single/choose mode) displaying Basic Pokémon in hand.
  - 1P & 2P Parity: In 1P mode, prompts user for `self` active then `opp` active sequentially. In 2P mode, both clients select concurrently; syncs via `startingActiveChosen` socket event and card move events.
  - Legality & Gating:
    - `shared/engine/rules/rules-state.mjs`: `canPerformAction` allows `moveCard` to `targetZoneId === 'active'` during `phase === 'setup'` or `turnNumber === 0` for both players; blocks all other game actions (energy attachment, supporters, attacks) before turn 1.
    - `shared/engine/reduce.mjs`: `validateLegality` allows `moveCard` from `hand` to `active` when active is empty during setup/turn 0 even when `state.turn.player !== playerId`.
    - `shouldAutoDrawAtTurnStart` guards with `turnNumber >= 1`, preventing opening turn draw during setup.
  - Added unit test suite `shared/engine/rules/__tests__/starting-active.test.mjs` (5 tests) and wired into `package.json` test script. 1856/1856 tests green across 58 suites.

S173 2026-09-18 feature (spike only, nothing shipped): PTCG Live-style preview panel for
  double-click on the active Pokémon. Throwaway renderer `panel-spike.mjs` + Playwright shots in
  out/ptcg-live-panel-*.png, drawn from live TCGdex data. User picked hybrid-no-crop layout and
  panel-with-attached-energy-slides; design gate not yet passed.

  empty on a card whose reference shows Tera rule text; ENERGY_SYMBOL_TO_TYPE in
  energy-effects.mjs is module-private but the panel needs it.
  flag: correction to S173's premise — attack-zone-geometry.js is NOT obsolete, it is load-bearing.
  The rendered pieces must OCCLUDE the printed text they replace, and `attackZoneBounds()` already
  gives the top of the printed attack box per attack/ability count. Bands are frame-specific: a Tera
  ex header is ~20% of the card, a classic frame's is not.
  Settled by user review across spike revisions v2→v20: the CARD SCAN IS THE BACKGROUND and stays
  visible; rendered pieces sit on top of it. HP pill covers the printed HP slot (live HP diverges
  from print); attack panels hover over the printed attack text box; weakness/resistance/retreat
  are three discrete flush tiles over the printed strip; the printed foot (illustrator, set number,
  ex-rule box) and the printed top (stage pill, name, Tera banner, evolves-from, rule text) are
  LEFT VISIBLE. No cream/opaque field anywhere — an earlier revision used one and the user rejected
  it twice for hiding the card.
  flag: my first two readings of "remove the top / remove the cream" were wrong in the same way — I
  replaced the print with an opaque band instead of uncovering the card. When the user says remove
  chrome here, they mean show the card, not substitute another fill.

S174 2026-09-19 design: wrote `.agent/designs/013-tcg-live-card-inspector.md` (status draft, gate
  pending) and finished the spike by adding the user's dim rule. Still nothing shipped in client/
  or shared/ — `panel-spike.mjs` only.
  Dim rule (user request, settled): payability comes from `listAttacks()` rather than a re-derivation,
  so the panel can never disagree with the engine. ≥1 attack payable → no card dim, unpayable panels
  alone recede; 0 payable → whole card box dims. Actions never dim — Retreat and Pass Turn stay legal
  at any energy count, so greying them would lie about the state.
  flag: first attempt used `opacity: .45` on the panel and it BROKE C1 — alpha makes the panel
  translucent, so the card's own printed "Bright Flame" / "Discard 2 Energy" ghosts back through the
  panel that exists to cover it (visible in v22/v23). `filter: saturate/brightness` dims without
  touching alpha and is clean (v24/v25). Recorded as 013 C2.
  Found while writing 013 — the blocking item the spike never exercised: **008 and 013 collide on the
  same gesture.** `click: imageClick` + `dblclick: doubleClick` are bound to the same nodes
  (card-listener-table.js:18-19), and 008 D1 put the attack preview on single-click. Double-clicking
  your own active therefore pops the attack preview, re-pops it on click #2, then tears it down for
  the carousel. Teardown is already correct (`openAttackPreview`'s `onClosed` runs cleanup, and
  `doubleClick` calls `closeCardPreview(null, true)`), so this is a visible flash rather than a leak.
  013 O1-C resolves it by folding 008's zones into the double-click inspector and returning
  single-click to select-to-move — one host per gesture, and it repairs 008's R3 (the selection
  capability D1 took away) for free. It REVISES user-confirmed D1/D6, so it must be signed at the
  gate rather than assumed; fallback is O1-A (debounce), explicitly not O1-B (accept the flash).
  flag: 013 O2 picks the carousel (`card-picker.js`) over the 008 pop host because double-click today
  already forks — attached cards → `openCarouselViewer`, otherwise `openCardPreview` — so the panel
  looked different depending on whether the Pokémon carried Energy. Mounting chrome via a new optional
  `decorate({node, holoWrapper, card, index})` hook keeps card-picker generic (it also serves the
  discard pile, deck, prizes and trainer pickers). Contract: the decorator must keep `holoWrapper`
  inside the returned node or `slideWrapper()` and the holo hover sync break.
  flag: eslint.config.mjs scopes `globals` to `**/*.js`, so every root `.mjs` fails `no-undef` on
  process/fetch/console/document — pre-existing, `verify.mjs` fails identically. Not worth fixing
  mid-spike; a real fix belongs in a config PR. Separately, prettier is not a direct dependency (only
  eslint-config-prettier + eslint-plugin-prettier), so `npx prettier` fails and the repo's own
  `format` script cannot resolve it — format with `npx eslint --fix`.
  Spike CLI now: `--attached "…"` drives dim state (3 Fire = clean, 2 = partial, 1 Grass = full dim);
  `resolveDetail` memoises so N copies of one Energy cost one lookup.

S179 2026-09-19 feature: TCG Live card inspector on double-click (PR #153, design 013).

  - Slice 2: carousel `decorate` hook in `card-picker.js` + `client/src/setup/rules/card-inspector.mjs` renderer + overlay styles.

S180 2026-09-19 feature: card inspector round 2 — abilities, content-sized text, working attack click (PR #153, design 013 D6–D11).
  - Abilities: the stack anchors on `abilityZoneBounds()` when the card has one and renders a panel that fires via
    `dispatchAuthoritativeUseAbility` under authority, `runAbilitySteps` locally. Rule-box text (Tera/Stellar) is excluded —
    TCGdex carries it in the same `abilities` array. Bench shows attacks inert WITHOUT dimming: the reason is positional,
    not an energy shortage, so receding them would lie. Fixes the reported "Charmander's Agile isnt shown".
  - Content-sized stack: the design had panels absorb slack with `flex: 1`, which on a one-attack card painted a foot of
    empty white over the artwork. Now anchored at `blockTopPct` with no bottom, sized by content, with the stat band pinned
    to the printed weakness strip independently — so the card shows through in the gap (user request).
  - flag: `closeCardInspector` was a bare alias for `teardownAll`, which only removed listeners and never closed the
    carousel. The attack dispatched into a modal still covering the board, which is exactly how "clicking does nothing"
    looks — the reported "attacks arent wired up" symptom. It closes the picker now. Per-panel `onclick` handlers were
    also replaced by ONE delegated listener on the wrap, so a REFRESH_EVENTS re-render that swaps the chrome subtree
    cannot silently drop a handler.
  - flag: found, NOT fixed — `passiveCostDiscount` (ability-executors.mjs) gates on `/(cost|energy)/` then returns 1
    unconditionally when no number matches, so Agile's "If this Pokémon has no Energy attached, it has no Weakness"
    grants −1 to every attack and Live Coal ({R}) reads payable with ZERO Energy. That is a rules-layer defect affecting
    combat legality, so it is left to the rules owner; the model stays faithful to the engine instead of disagreeing with
    it, and `card-inspector-model.test.mjs` pins the consequence in a test that names it a quirk.
  - flag: the branch had moved under me — another session pushed ~30 commits and merged origin/main in, so the first push
    was rejected. Merged rather than force-pushed (that would have destroyed their work): 10.7k lines, including
    `card-picker.js`, `rules-state.mjs`, `ability-executors.mjs` and `package.json`. Verified afterwards that the
    `decorate` hook, both exports and both test-file entries survived, and the suite is 1930/1930.
  - The pre-existing `trainer drop: Trainer without synced effect text` failure is GONE — fixed by the concurrent
    server-authoritative parser/reducer sweep. Still NOT browser-verified: the worktree has no node_modules (server dies on
    ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND socket.io) and :4000 serves the primary checkout.
  - flag: four designs now claim the number 013 (`013-tcg-live-card-inspector`, `013-grand-tree-server-evolve`,
    `013-server-attack-damage-scaling`, `013-server-turn-order-coin-call`). This design's decision landed as D53 because
    concurrent work took D50–D52. `.agent/designs/` numbering needs a reconciliation pass.

S181 2026-09-19 patch: fixed `passiveCostDiscount` misreading ability text (design 013 D12, closes D9).
  - Root cause: the guard was `if (!/(cost|energy)/.test(t)) return 0;` followed by an unconditional `return 1` when no
    number matched — so ANY ability that merely mentioned Energy or cost granted a free symbol off every attack.
    Charmander's Agile ("If this Pokémon has no Energy attached, it has no Weakness") made its Live Coal ({R}) payable with
    ZERO Energy attached. Evidence: `passiveCostDiscount(Agile) = 1` vs `0` for a card with no ability.
  - Fix at the narrowest layer (that one function): require an explicit reduction verb
    (`less|fewer|reduc|decrease|lower`) instead of assuming one. Two extra narrowings fell out of writing the proof —
    a retreat-cost wording with no attack mention is now declined, because `parseRetreatCostModifier` owns it and reading
    it here would have discounted ATTACKS off a retreat ability; and `fewer N` now parses its amount, which the amount
    regexes had missed (`(\d+)\s+less` only).
  - Tests: five in `rules-extended.test.mjs` (the file that owns the function) covering Agile, the retreat wording,
    cost-without-a-reduction-verb, and three genuine reductions; plus the end-to-end assertion in
    `card-inspector-model.test.mjs`, flipped from "pins the quirk" to expecting `payable: false` / `dimLevel: 'full'`.
    Focused proof 371/371, full gate 1934/1934, no failures.
  - flag: the whole-path test only existed because the panel displayed the bug faithfully rather than hiding it. Worth
    keeping that property in mind — a "known quirk" test that documents wrong behaviour is a good place to look for the
    next real bug.
  - Still NOT browser-verified: the worktree has no node_modules, and :4000 serves the primary checkout.

S183 2026-09-19 patch: fixed the 2P server-authoritative pre-turn softlock. The Starting Active
prompt was gated on a fixed 2.5s sleep, so it opened against an empty hand, hit its no-candidates
early-return, and nothing could ever re-open it — leaving rulesState pinned at phase 'setup' /
turnNumber 0, where canPerformAction and validateLegality deny every action silently. Now gated on
the hand itself (waitForOpeningHand: event-driven, bounded) with the watcher able to re-arm the
prompt; and under server authority the redundant local deal + mulligan pass is skipped, since
setupGame() already shuffled, dealt and adjudicated mulligans before it broadcast. Files:
client/src/setup/rules/rules-bridge.js, package.json.
  flag: PR #152's own regression test (client/src/setup/netcode/__tests__/multiplayer-starting-active.test.mjs)
  was never added to package.json's explicit test list, so it had never run. Now wired in.
  flag: e2e-options.test.mjs carries 11 pre-existing failures (it imports only e2e-options.mjs and
  rules-state.mjs, both untouched by S183). pnpm lint still fails on pre-existing no-empty/prettier.
  flag: .agent/{STATE,DECISIONS,journal/2026-09}.md arrived with unresolved merge conflict markers
  predating S183. STATE rebuilt from git per the manual's "trust git" rule; this journal and
  DECISIONS were appended to only, never rewritten. Wants a deliberate harness-conflict cleanup.
  flag: S183 correction — I first wrote the 2P harness renderer wedge as "an environment artifact,
  not the S183 defect". Not proven: the URL-capture probe's dump was deleted before it finished, so
  the 113 failing requests were never identified. Downgraded to an inference in STATE. The wedge is
  orthogonal to the S183 fix (which came from source reading, not from those probes), but it is an
  unexplained hang and should be re-checked on a networked machine.

S184 2026-09-19 debug: 'stacked cards not draggable' = a hand MutationObserver livelock. reconcileHandStacks
re-appended every #hand child unconditionally; appendChild mutates even when the child is already last, and
hand-observer.js watches #hand childList and calls it back, so each pass scheduled the next forever and no
dragstart could ever fire. Files: client/src/setup/zones/hand-stack-dom.js (+1 regression test),
client/src/css/self-containers.css, client/src/css/opp-containers.css.
  flag: Closes S183's unexplained 2P harness wedge -- it was this livelock, not a 404 image storm or a sandbox artifact.
  flag: Needs no duplicates: any non-empty #hand wedges, so PR #151 shipped broken for every hand. Design 014's
  'Live verification: Playwright' row was skipped.
  flag: Also fixed dragStart's .high-zIndex (z-index: 1 !important) sinking the grabbed card behind its own back
  copies -- new rule .hand-card-stack > .dragging { z-index: 30 !important }.
  flag: The isReconciling latch guards only synchronous re-entry; it cannot stop an observer microtask. Any future
  childList write inside reconcileHandStacks relives this.

with {auto:true,tilt:false} swept again — cause confirmed by toggling one parameter. Files:

S188 2026-09-19 patch: 'the foil is still frozen, everywhere' — the OS `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce` gate zeroed
the drift amplitude for BOTH modes, and this machine reports reduce (Windows SPI_GETCLIENTAREAANIMATION 0x1042 = 0),
so every foil held the neutral pose (--pointer-x 38%). Gate removed, amplitude now unconditional (D56). Files:
client/src/setup/deck-builder/core/holo.mjs + its __tests__/holo.test.mjs. PR #165.
  flag: Playwright's DEFAULT `reducedMotion` is the 'no-preference' EMULATION, so it masked the real OS value —
  S186's "the mat sweeps" measurement was an artifact of that, and PR #164's body carries the wrong claim
  (corrected in #165's). Use `reducedMotion: null` and read matchMedia INSIDE the app page, never on about:blank.
  flag: #164 merged mid-flight (S187), so the follow-up commit pushed to fix/holo-idle-drift was ORPHANED by that
  merge; re-applied as 1d9d725 onto the #164 merge commit and opened as PR #165 (main 143d3a6, mergeable clean).
  flag: the old watch-out "if the foil is frozen, check prefers-reduced-motion FIRST" is now INVERTED — the holo
  must never consult matchMedia again, because a re-added gate freezes every card on this machine.
  flag: live proof under the real setting (emulation off, page reports reduce=true): mat/board foil 18.1%→33.5%
  over 2.8s and the carousel slide moving; the fixture's hand held no holo card, so hand rests on the shared
  hydrateHolo auto path + the unit tests.

S189 2026-09-19 debug: "attack window doesn't operate" = the carousel's `stage.setPointerCapture()` retargets the
click to the stage, so the design-013 inspector's delegated listener on `.ptcg-inspector` (a stage descendant)
never fires — a silent dead click (no console error, no battle-log line, no emit; inspector stays open). Fix:
`.ptcg-chrome` added to `isSwipeBlockedTarget` (`client/src/setup/image-logic/card-picker.js`) so pointerdown over
the inspector skips capture and the click is delivered to the attack/ability panel. Files: card-picker.js,
test-card-inspector-e2e.mjs (step 9 now real input), package.json (`test:inspector`).
  flag: the prior "working attack click" (S180) and "ability dead click" fix (4b73101) were only ever proven with
  programmatic `el.click()` — which dispatches no pointerdown, so capture is never set and the click bubbles
  normally. That is why both passed while a real mouse click was dead; 4b73101's "the hint was refused" theory was
  never the whole story. Any carousel-interaction gate must be verified with real input.
  flag: RED/GREEN toggled on the real-input harness — without `.ptcg-chrome` step 13 "clicking attack panel closes
  inspector" FAILS; with it ALL PASS. pnpm test 1965/1965. Lint on the touched files shows only pre-existing
  CRLF/`no-undef`/`no-console` noise.
  flag: the S189 fix is uncommitted on `main` alongside the still-uncommitted S188 holo, S183 rules-bridge and S184
  hand-stack-dom work — none of it is on a branch/PR yet.

S190 2026-09-19 debug: "Dive Ball shows every Pokémon, not just Water" = `parseSearchDeckParams` only understood the
energy-symbol form (`Basic {W} Pokémon`); a word-form type qualifier (`a Water Pokémon`) fell through to the generic
`what: 'Pokémon'`, so `matchesSearch` matched every Pokémon. Fix: parse word-form typed search clauses into
`<Type> Pokémon` / `Basic <Type> Pokémon` and enforce it in `matchesSearch` via the existing
`pokemonMatchesEnergyType`. Also fixes Telepathic Psychic Energy's `Basic Psychic Pokémon` bench search. Files:
shared/engine/rules/{search-match,trainer-effects}.mjs + trainer-effects.test.mjs (2 tests). PR #167.
  flag: reproduced first — Dive Ball parsed `what: 'Pokémon'` and matched Water/Fire/Lightning; after the fix only the
  Water Pokémon survives. pnpm test 1967/1967 (1965 + 2 new); lint on the touched files is the usual pre-existing
  CRLF/prettier noise (no new rule violations).

  turn hooks use `isGameConcluded`. 1.3b sudden death: `setupGame({ firstPrizeWins })` + first
  `takePrizes` ends it (scope: no automatic fresh mat). 1.4 server enforces legacy Mega/Primal
  turn-end on evolve via `requiresTurnEndOnEvolve` with the zone's attached Spirit Link.

  drag.js: added an explicit host-level drop-target path - [data-drop-zone]/[data-drop-user]
  via explicitDropZoneOf(); dragOver highlights only the matching side; dragLeave/dragEnd/drop
  clear it; drop() resolves dZoneId from the attribute and REFUSES a user mismatch (cannot
  fling the opponent's card into your Lost Zone). lost-zone-panel.js marks both sides
  data-drop-zone=lostZone + data-drop-user and reuses the board's dragOver/dragLeave/drop, so a
  rail drop is exactly an in-mat drop (same moveCardBundle/netcode path); thumbs set draggable
  =false; index.css .lost-zone-rail-drop highlight. Rail test 11/11; full suite 2441/2441;

S214 2026-09-20 fix(netcode): closed I47 — eliminated opening hand 9-card overdraw and setup race under server authority.
  Root causes:
  1. Under SERVER_AUTHORITATIVE=1, server `setupGame()` deals 7 cards to hand, 6 to prizes, and authoritatively
     draws 1 card for starter on turn 1 start (starter hand: 8 cards, defender: 7 cards). However, client's
     `hookTurnStartDraw` and `shouldEmitTurnStartDraw` in `rules-bridge.js` lacked a guard for `serverAuthoritative`,
     so on turn 1 transition client dispatched a redundant `draw()` command via `moveCard(deck, hand)`, inflating
     starter's hand to 9 cards on the server.
  2. Race condition: `maybeBeginServerTurnOrder()` had `&& !isE2eMode()` bypassing `openingSetupReadyForCoinFlip`,
     allowing `beginSetupWithTurnOrder()` -> `drawOpeningHand()` to execute concurrently with `setupPrizes()`
     before dealOrder settled, reducing deck from 20 to 13 cards and breaking deal order validation.
  Fix:
  - Guarded `hookTurnStartDraw` and `shouldEmitTurnStartDraw` against `systemState.serverAuthoritative`.
  - Removed `!isE2eMode()` bypass so `maybeBeginServerTurnOrder()` strictly waits for `openingSetupReadyForCoinFlip`.
  - Aligned `flip-gate-test.mjs` with Design 013 (coin call auto-handled in E2E mode without DOM overlay, asserted
    starter has 8 cards with turn-1 draw and defender has 7 cards).
  Tests: 2533/2533 unit tests pass; `flip-gate-test.mjs` and `test-card-inspector-e2e.mjs` ALL PASS against authoritative server.

S227 2026-09-20 debug: retreat split an evolution stack into two side-by-side cards.
  Cause: `applyRetreatSwap` (reduce.mjs) reorders each stack - it pushes the attached top
  Evolution before its Basic root - and `apply-view.js`'s `placeCardInZone` only attaches into a
  host `.play-container` that already exists, so on that first render the attachment fell through
  to the top-level play-zone branch and got its own container (stack rendered as two cards).
  Fix: `applyView` places every root before its attachments (`placementOrder`). Repro confirmed at
  the reducer (post-retreat array order) and at the DOM layer (regression test red before the fix).
  Verified: pnpm test 2600/2600. Files: client/src/setup/netcode/apply-view.js,
  client/src/setup/netcode/__tests__/apply-view.test.mjs.

S229 2026-09-28 debug->patch: Mega Greninja ex "Mortal Shuriken" (server authority) discarded ANY hand card and never opened the damage mat-pick.
  Root cause (2, both server-only): (1) parseAbility's discardCostAbility emitted {basic, energyType} but
  executor.mjs's discard-cost filter reads {energyOnly, basicOnly, energyTypes} — both unset, so every hand
  card passed. (2) moveDamageAbility had no case in executeSteps / no EXTRA_STEP_HANDLERS entry, so the
  damage step fell to `default: break` (no PendingChoice, no damage).
  Fix: abilities.mjs discardCostAbility now emits energyOnly (scoped to the discard clause) + basicOnly +
  energyTypes; trainer-steps.mjs maps moveDamageAbility -> damageCounters (opponent/own per onOpponent).
  Verified: repro offered [Water, Fire, Trainer, Froakie] and no 2nd prompt before; after: [Water Energy] then
  damage picker over opp active+bench -> 60 dmg. Regression in ability-execution.test.mjs; pnpm test 2601/2601.
  Files: shared/engine/rules/abilities.mjs, shared/engine/effects/trainer-steps.mjs,
  shared/engine/__tests__/ability-execution.test.mjs.
  flag: client rules-bridge discard-cost ignores `energyOnly`, so "discard a card … draw" abilities (e.g.
  Gather Materials) still restrict the picker to Energy — pre-existing, unfiled.
